- XACA-0651: Consumer `aiteamforge doctor` + fresh-install gaps (both tap-native, no canonical mirror). **Fix 1 — false `LCARS health check script missing` FAILURE (`libexec/commands/aiteamforge-doctor.sh`, `check_launchagents`):** the script-present check referenced bare `${AITEAMFORGE_DIR}`, which is unset in the doctor context on consumers, so the path collapsed to `/lcars-health-check.sh` (root) and reported a FAILURE even when the script was correctly installed at `${working_dir}/lcars-health-check.sh`. Now resolves the path via `get_working_dir` like its six sibling checks (`AITEAMFORGE_DIR:-$HOME/aiteamforge`). **Fix 2 — spurious `Dev-team not a git repository` warning (`check_git`):** a consumer tap install lays `${working_dir}` (e.g. `~/aiteamforge`) down as a plain product dir with no `.git` — expected and healthy, not a fault. The check was dev-source-of-truth-centric and warned on every consumer; now it gates the clean/dirty checks on `.git` presence and emits an informational `print_info` note on consumers instead of a warning. **Fix 3 — kanban-backup LaunchAgent not loaded on fresh install (`libexec/installers/install-kanban.sh`, `install_backup_launchagent`):** the agent is `RunAtLoad=true` and redirects stdout/stderr into `~/aiteamforge-backups/kanban/` — a directory only created by `install_kanban_backup`, which early-returns when the backup script source is missing — so launchd could not open the job's StandardOutPath and the agent never registered (the lcars-health agent logs to `/tmp` and always loads, the exact asymmetry observed). Now: (a) `mkdir -p` the log dir in the loader so the load is self-sufficient; (b) guard the `{{BACKUP_INTERVAL}}` substitution against an empty/non-numeric value that would render an invalid `<integer></integer>` plist, falling back to the documented `900`s default; (c) verify registration via `launchctl list` after load rather than trusting the legacy `launchctl load` exit code (which returns 0 even on reject), surfacing an accurate warning + manual-load hint on failure. **Sibling-drift alignment (XACA-0651-009, PR #559 review):** `install_lcars_health_launchagent` and `install_lcars_watch_launchagent` shared the same blind `if launchctl load; then` success-trust pattern; both now use `launchctl load || true` + a `launchctl list` registration check so neither inherits the false-success bug if it ever gains a non-`/tmp` log path or RunAtLoad behavior. Regression test `tests/test-xaca-0651-consumer-doctor-gaps.sh` (22 cases, incl. sibling-drift anti-regression).

- XACA-0609: Lowercase `PROJECTID` for legal-startup session names (`share/scripts/teams/legal-startup.sh`, mirror of canonical root `legal-startup.sh`). `legal-startup.sh` built tmux session names from the raw `$PROJECTID`, so they only matched the canonical instance id `legal-coparenting` by accident when the id was already lowercase. Added `PROJECT_LOWER=$(echo "$PROJECTID" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')` + `SESSION_PREFIX="legal-${PROJECT_LOWER}"` and routed all session-name uses (7 `terminals[]` keys, `terminal_order[]`, `session_name=`, both `start_lcars_server` args, `lcars-target.js` append, `open_lcars_tab` arg, `resolve_lcars_port`) through `${SESSION_PREFIX}` — parity with `finance-startup.sh`/`medical-startup.sh`. Also unified the LCARS port fallback to hash `${PROJECT_LOWER}` instead of the constant string `"legal"` (distinct legal projects now get distinct fallback ports); legal's own port band `8220 + cksum % 20` is unchanged. Raw `PROJECTID` preserved for the directory path, exported env vars, and log filenames. Mirror of canonical root `legal-startup.sh` via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0658: Versioned release-push flow — config-driven version bump + verify gate + RELNOTES promotion + branch-push auto-promotion (`share/scripts/kb-release-version-bump`, `share/scripts/kb-release-version-gate`, `share/scripts/kb-release-push-promote`, `share/scripts/lib/kb-relnotes-promote.sh` [all NEW], `share/kanban-hooks/aiteamforge_paths.py`, `share/lcars-ui/server.py`, `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters `lcars.js?v=3.52`→`?v=3.53`, `lcars.css?v=32.22`→`?v=32.23`). A generic, per-team-configurable release-push pipeline. **Config (optional `team-paths.json` keys, backward-compatible):** `version_sources[]` (`{platform, file, match_pattern, write_pattern, encoding, replace_all}`), `relnotes_sources[]` (`{platform, dir}`), and `branch_env_map` (branch→env; accepts a string `"develop":"DEV"` = all release platforms OR a per-platform object `"develop":{"ios":"DEV"}`). New `aiteamforge_paths.py` accessors `get_team_version_sources`/`get_team_branch_env_map`/`get_team_relnotes_sources`/`normalize_branch_env`. **Auto-bump** (`kb-release-version-bump`): writes a release's `platformVersions.<plat>` target into the configured file(s); honors `replace_all`; idempotent; introduces a generic plain-text `VERSION` convention for platforms lacking a native version file. **Verify gate** (`kb-release-version-gate`): hard-blocks (exit 1) on code-version ≠ release-target; non-PROD + unset target = warn, PROD + unset target = FAIL; persists `platforms.<plat>.gateStatus` to the board via `POST /api/releases/<id>/platform-gate-status` (mirrors through XACA-0659's `_sync_release_metadata_to_manifest`), rendered read-only as a PASS/FAIL/SKIP badge on the LCARS release platform card with a >24h stale marker. **RELNOTES** (`lib/kb-relnotes-promote.sh`, sourceable): copies + header-rewrites RELNOTES between envs, backs up an existing destination, lays down the store-asset STUB at PROD (no LLM copy from a shell context), auto-commits non-fatally. **Branch-push auto-promotion** (`kb-release-push-promote` + `kb-git-push` wrapper): resolves branch→env, disambiguates multiple active releases, runs the gate as a hard block, promotes via the `/promote` endpoint, then promotes RELNOTES; PROD advances stage + stub but never auto-generates store copy (prints a loud manual `/relnotes` + `/cr` prompt); LCARS-down and already-at-env are non-fatal no-ops. 115 tests (86 pytest + 29 zsh). Mirror of canonical `scripts/`, `kanban-hooks/`, `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0657: Bidirectional CR ↔ Release assignment + suppress create-CR prompt when linked (`share/scripts/kb-cr.sh`, `share/lcars-ui/server.py`, `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars-cr-tab.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars-cr-tab.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters `lcars.js?v=3.51`→`?v=3.52`, `lcars-cr-tab.js`→`?v=1.1`, `lcars.css?v=32.21`→`?v=32.22`, `lcars-cr-tab.css`→`?v=1.1`). Adds a bidirectional link between Change Requests and Releases. **Data model:** a CR record gains `releaseAssignment {releaseId, releaseName, assignedAt}`; a release object gains `linkedCRs [{crId, crTitle, linkedAt}]`; the release manifest gains `crIds[]`. Absent = unlinked (no mass migration; readers default cleanly). **Helpers (`kb-cr assign-release`/`unassign-release`, `kb-release link-cr`/`unlink-cr`):** all four entry points delegate to one shared `_kb_cr_release_link`/`_kb_cr_release_unlink` pair (sibling-drift prevention) that writes all three sites; title/name snapshots captured at link time; re-link unlinks the old release first; idempotent. **Server API:** `POST /api/releases/<id>/crs` (body `{crId}`) and `DELETE /api/releases/<id>/crs/<crId>` with CR/release existence validation and an OPTIONAL platform match (non-blocking warning in the response, link still proceeds); the endpoint writes byte-identical board structure to the shell helpers (verified by a parity diff in testing — which caught and fixed a manifest-path divergence: flat `<id>.json` vs the canonical `<id>/manifest.json` subdir, plus two `updatedAt` stamp mismatches). **Release Manager skill:** the PROD-promote step now inspects `linkedCRs` and skips the `/cr` create suggestion when a CR is already linked (prose + workflow example kept consistent). **LCARS UI:** the CR tab row shows its linked Release (clickable → Releases section) and the release card shows linked CR chips (clickable → CHANGE REQ section), reusing the existing `switchSection` + highlight-pulse nav. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` + `scripts/kb-cr.sh` changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0660: k501 sibling-drift NOTE in `share/scripts/deploy-worktree-personas.sh` (comment-only mirror of canonical `scripts/deploy-worktree-personas.sh`). dev-team's `kb-sync-personas sync-worktrees` gained a loop that also deploys personas into the nested main git worktree (e.g. `<container>/develop/`) for container-layout teams (Firebase/Android). That fix is intentionally NOT replicated in this script — it deploys from the tap installation path (`~/aiteamforge/.../personas/agents/`), unavailable on dev-source machines, so it cannot read the dev-team master the nested-main-root deploy requires. On tap machines `kb-sync-personas sync-worktrees` is authoritative for nested-main-root deployment. A NOTE comment documents this at the gap site. No functional change.
- XACA-0659: Release `manifest.json` write-through on update + promote (`share/lcars-ui/server.py`, mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/server.py`). `handle_update_release` and `handle_promote_release` previously wrote release changes only to the authoritative board `releases[]`, leaving each release's per-release `manifest.json` carrying permanently stale top-level scalars (`name`, `version`, `versionCode`, `currentEnvironment`). New helper `_sync_release_metadata_to_manifest` mirrors board-authoritative values into the manifest after every save — top-level metadata (`name`/`shortTitle`/`type`/`status`/`targetDate`), the full `platforms` object, and legacy flat scalars (`version`/`versionCode`=buildNumber/`currentEnvironment`) derived from a representative platform (single platform → that platform; multi-platform → first key alphabetically for determinism). A `_source` marker stamps the file as a derived mirror. Manifest-sync failures are logged and never abort the board write. New test suite `share/lcars-ui/tests/test_xaca0659_release_metadata_sync.py` (33 tests). Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`. (CLI-side `kb-release promote` verb + `--platform-build` flag ship in dev-team's non-tap `kanban-helpers.sh`.)

## [0.13.4] - 2026-06-09

- XACA-0652: Durable LCARS server launch + shared iterm2 venv bootstrap for cockpit scripts. **Durable server launch (`share/scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh`):** `start_lcars_server` now launches `server.py` via `nohup env ... sh -c '... exec python3 ...'` with `disown`, ensuring the server survives the startup script's exit on tap-installed machines (e.g. M4Mini) where the parent shell exits and would previously SIGHUP the server process group. `exec` inside `sh -c` replaces `sh` so `$!` remains the python PID. Readiness check hardened: after the first `/api/status 200`, a 0.3s pause + re-check confirms persistence. **Shared iterm2 bootstrap (`share/scripts/iterm2_venv_bootstrap.py`, NEW):** the tap-venv probe/re-exec logic from XACA-0650's `iterm2_window_manager.py` is extracted into a standalone module added to the `sync-tap.sh` mirror map and deployed by both the parametric and rendered-template install paths in `libexec/installers/install-team.sh`. **Sibling cockpit scripts fixed (`share/lcars-ui/iterm-browser.py`, `share/scripts/iterm2_window_manager.py`):** both now import the shared bootstrap module instead of inline probes. `set-lcars-profile-browser.py` and `create-agent-panel-profile.py` are unchanged (they do not import iterm2). `iterm2_window_manager.py` uses a two-candidate path search (same-dir for tap layout, scripts/ subdir for dev-source layout). `iterm-browser.py` uses `../scripts` which resolves correctly in both layouts. Upgrade auto-coverage: `update_runtime_helpers` already sweeps `share/scripts/*.py` so the bootstrap will be refreshed on `brew upgrade` once it is installed. **PR #551 review fix — lazy brew probe:** `ensure_iterm2_venv()` previously ran two `brew --prefix` subprocess calls at module-import time unconditionally. Inverted to fast-path first: `importlib.util.find_spec("iterm2")` is checked at the top of `ensure_iterm2_venv()` — if iterm2 is already importable (the common case when running under the tap venv or after re-exec), the function returns immediately with zero subprocess calls. The `brew --prefix` candidate probe is now inside a lazy `_get_tap_venv_candidates()` helper, only called when `find_spec` returns None. The execv infinite-loop guard is implicit: after re-exec the new python3 has iterm2, so fast-path returns before reaching the candidates. Tap mirror synced (`share/scripts/iterm2_venv_bootstrap.py`).

## [0.13.3] - 2026-06-08

- XACA-0650: Fix stale `~/aiteamforge/.venv` remediation message in `iterm2_window_manager.py` and add tap-venv dep check to `aiteamforge doctor`. **Fix 1 (canonical `share/scripts/iterm2_window_manager.py`):** the docstring and the `ImportError` handler both previously told users to provision `~/aiteamforge/.venv` — a path deprecated when XACA-0175 moved Python deps to the tap-owned venv at `$HOMEBREW_PREFIX/var/aiteamforge/venv`. Updated docstring and error messages to point at the correct venv location and instruct `brew postinstall aiteamforge` as the fix. The `ImportError` handler now resolves venv candidates in priority order: `brew --prefix`-derived path → `/opt/homebrew/var/aiteamforge/venv` → `/usr/local/var/aiteamforge/venv` (covers both Apple Silicon and Intel Macs). The deprecated `~/aiteamforge/.venv` and `~/dev-team/.venv` candidates are removed. Canonical change in `dev-team/iterm2_window_manager.py`; mirror via `sync-tap.sh`. **Fix 2 (`libexec/commands/aiteamforge-doctor.sh`, tap-native):** `aiteamforge doctor` previously had no visibility into the tap-owned venv or its required deps — running it while `iterm2` was missing reported 1 unrelated failure and missed the gap entirely. Added `check_python_venv()` function that sources `libexec/lib/python-env.sh` (already shipped by XACA-0175) to resolve `AITEAMFORGE_PYTHON`, then checks presence and executability of the venv Python, pip, and each required package (`iterm2`, `pyzipper`). Each missing package is a `fail` with remediation `brew postinstall aiteamforge`. The new check is exposed as `aiteamforge doctor --check python-venv` and is wired into the `all` pass between `dependencies` and `framework`. **PR #550 review fixes:** (1) `check_python_venv()` now dynamically parses package names from `share/requirements.txt` (strips comments, blank lines, and version pins `pkg==x.y → pkg`) — no static array; adding a dep to `requirements.txt` is the single required change and doctor will check it automatically; falls back to the known `("iterm2" "pyzipper")` set when the file is unreadable; new `tests/test-xaca-0650-doctor-venv.sh` (17 tests) asserts the k501 anti-regression case. (2) Removed redundant `import subprocess as _sp` alias from the `except ImportError` block in `share/scripts/iterm2_window_manager.py` — `subprocess` is imported at module level; the inner alias was unused noise. (3) Removed redundant `2>&1` from `&>/dev/null 2>&1` in `check_python_venv` (`&>` captures both streams; trailing redirect was a no-op). **PR #550 [Review] hardening (XACA-0650-014):** requirements.txt parser now handles all PEP 508 version-specifier operators (`>=`, `<=`, `~=`, `!=`, `>`, `<`) and extras/env-marker syntax — strips from the first operator/`[`/`;` char (`"${line%%[<>=~!;[]*}"`), plus full leading-and-trailing whitespace trim via parameter expansion double-pass; previously only `==` was stripped and `%%[[:space:]]*` only removed trailing content (so `  pkg` produced an empty name and was silently dropped). Test suite extended from 17 to 23 cases: `>=`/`~=`/`<=` pin variants, indented package line, extras spec (`pkg[foo]`), and environment-marker line each assert the correct bare name is checked.

- XACA-0649: Consumer shell resolver `_kb_get_kanban_dir` — sibling-drift fix + upgrade refresh + quarantine python3 guard (PR #549 review). **Scope expanded** after initial PR review found the fix was incomplete for the upgrade path. (1) **kanban-helpers.template.sh** (`share/templates/kanban/kanban-helpers.template.sh`): applied the same three-tier fix as `kanban-aliases.sh`. The upgrade path (`update_shell_helpers` in `aiteamforge-upgrade.sh`) renders `~/aiteamforge/kanban-helpers.sh` from this template — NOT from `kanban-aliases.sh` — so the old early-exit guard `if [[ -d "${_atf_dir}/kanban" ]]` at line ~82 regressed the fix on every `brew upgrade`. Replaced with the same Strategy 1/2/3 structure (team-paths.json registry via python3 → well-known case arms → `$AITEAMFORGE_DIR/kanban` catch-all). Template placeholder tokens (`{{AITEAMFORGE_DIR}}`, `{{SHARED_DEV_ROOT}}`, `{{ORG_NAME}}`) are preserved. Runtime authority: fresh install uses `kanban-aliases.sh` (preferred by `install_kanban_helpers`); upgrade uses `kanban-helpers.template.sh` (rendered by `update_shell_helpers`). Added SIBLING-DRIFT NOTE comment to prevent future divergence. (2) **aiteamforge-upgrade.sh**: `update_shell_helpers` now prefers `kanban-aliases.sh` as the source for `kanban-helpers.sh` on upgrade (mirroring `install_kanban_helpers`'s preference), falling back to `kanban-helpers.template.sh` — so a future fix to `kanban-aliases.sh` is automatically picked up by upgrades without a separate template edit. Applies the same sed substitutions for all three placeholder tokens. (3) **kb-init-team-guard.sh** (`share/scripts/kb-init-team-guard.sh`, mirrored via `sync-tap.sh`): `_kb_detect_and_quarantine_stub` no longer silently treats `item_count` as 0 when python3 is unavailable. The old comment "python3 always available on macOS/Homebrew" was not a guard — if python3 was absent, `item_count` stayed `0` and a non-empty stub could be moved. Fixed: explicit `command -v python3` pre-check with early `return 1` and a clear operator message; also added `[[ "$item_count" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]` guard to treat non-numeric python3 output as non-empty (safe). (4) **Parity test** (`tests/test-xaca-0649-kanban-dir-resolver.sh`): extended from 7 to 14 cases — Cases 8–14 are exact mirrors of Cases 1–7 but source `kanban-helpers.template.sh` instead of `kanban-aliases.sh`, asserting both deployed copies exhibit the same correct behaviour for the `$AITEAMFORGE_DIR/kanban` coexistence scenario. A future edit to one file that is not mirrored in the other will fail here. All 14 cases pass. (5) **Initial PR fix** (as originally shipped): `kanban-aliases.sh` (`share/templates/aliases/kanban-aliases.sh`) — early-exit guard removed; `kb-init-team-guard.sh` gains `_kb_detect_and_quarantine_stub` for startup auto-quarantine.

## [0.13.2] - 2026-06-08

- XACA-0648: `share/scripts/kb-init-team` auto-detects tap-consumer machines and runs in LOCAL-ONLY mode (provisioning sites 12–14 only: board JSON, directory tree, knowledge INDEX files). Previously the script crashed with `FileNotFoundError` on first-launch of a parameterized team (e.g. `legal-coparenting`) because it assumed `homebrew-tap/libexec/lib/aiteamforge-paths.sh` existed relative to its location — which is only true on the dev-source-of-truth machine. Detection: the canonical `${DEV_TEAM_ROOT}/homebrew-tap/libexec/lib/aiteamforge-paths.sh` present → FULL mode (sites 1–14, unchanged); absent → LOCAL-ONLY mode (sites 12–14 only, registration sites 1–11 logged as cleanly skipped with "product-managed (tap install)"). Board-template resolver tries (a) dev path, (b) `brew --prefix aiteamforge` Cellar path, (c) `share/templates/kanban/` relative path, then falls back to an embedded heredoc so board creation never fails regardless of install layout. `--check-only` in LOCAL-ONLY mode skips registration sites without error and checks only board existence. `--dry-run` and `--non-interactive` work correctly in both modes. Team code is optional in LOCAL-ONLY mode (placeholder used; never written). PR #548 review fixes: (1) BSD mktemp blocker — trailing `.json` suffix in the mktemp template defeated randomization on macOS/BSD, causing "File exists" on the second provision attempt (first call creates a LITERAL file `...XXXXXX.json`, second call errors); fixed by using `"${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/kb-init-team-board-template.XXXXXX"` (X's at end, no suffix, honor TMPDIR); (2) tmpfile leak under `set -euo pipefail` — if python3 failed inside `_create_board_json` the `rm -f "$_tmp"` cleanup was never reached; fixed with a `trap ... RETURN` that fires on all exit paths including errors; detection pattern changed from a hardcoded `/tmp` path prefix to a `basename` check so it works regardless of TMPDIR value; (3) k501 sibling-drift in `scripts/kb-freelance` — applied matching mktemp fix and embedded fallback so freelance consumer provisioning also never fails on a missing template (mirrors kb-init-team). Canonical change in `dev-team`; mirror of `scripts/kb-init-team` via `sync-tap.sh` (`scripts/kb-freelance` is dev-tree only, not tap-mirrored).
- XACA-0647: Canonical-guard no longer flags personal-only machine configs (academy + personal teams such as finance-personal/legal-coparenting/medical-general, no platform team) as corrupt; only academy-alone/empty triggers re-seed. The old heuristic demanded academy PLUS at least one of ios/android/firebase/dns, wrongly re-seeding fleet-plan personal machines with the Main Event roster. The validity rule is consolidated into a single shared helper `teams_satisfy_canonical_guard()` in `share/kanban-hooks/aiteamforge_paths.py`, called by both `load_config()` and `share/lcars-ui/server.py` `_build_team_kanban_dirs()` — ending the two-site sibling drift (k501); the obsolete `CANONICAL_AT_LEAST_ONE_TEAMS` / `_CANONICAL_AT_LEAST_ONE` / `_CANONICAL_REQUIRED` constants were removed. Canonical change in `dev-team`; mirror via `sync-tap.sh`. (XACA-0457 follow-on)
- XACA-0646: Startup guard accepts a well-formed EMPTY board (`share/scripts/kb-init-team-guard.sh`, mirrored via `sync-tap.sh`). `_kb_board_is_present` previously defined a board as usable only when `backlog` was non-empty (`len(backlog) > 0`), so a freshly-provisioned team — whose board is rendered from `board-template.json` with `"backlog": []` — was always flagged uninitialized, and `kb_ensure_team_initialized` prompted the operator to re-provision on the team's very first launch (TTY) or warned on every headless startup. The fix redefines "usable" as **structurally valid**: a JSON object with a `backlog` list (empty OK), a non-empty `team` string (proves template substitution ran), and a `nextId` key. Both detection paths are patched together (k501 sibling-drift): the python3 check swaps `len(backlog) > 0` for the four-field structural predicate, and the grep fallback (python3-absent only) swaps its item-id grep — which necessarily failed for an empty board — for three portable `grep` tests (`"backlog"`, `"nextId"`, non-empty `"team"`). Corrupt/partial files (`{}`, `{"backlog":[]}` lacking team/nextId, non-list backlog, empty team) remain correctly NOT usable. Function/file-header comments, contract RETURN CODES, and the non-interactive/interactive prompt messages updated to match. Canonical change in `dev-team`; mirror of `scripts/kb-init-team-guard.sh` via `sync-tap.sh`.

## [0.13.1] - 2026-06-07

- XACA-0645: Group Roadmap Details & Reference entries by lifecycle (`share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters `lcars.js?v=3.50`→`?v=3.51`, `lcars.css?v=32.20`→`?v=32.21`). Frontend-only follow-on to XACA-0644 (no `/api/roadmap` change — the `state` enum is already derived server-side by `_derive_epic_roadmap_state` / `_derive_release_state`). Within both the Epics and Releases groups, entries are now bucketed by their derived `state` (`ACTIVE | PLANNED | ARCHIVED`) into **Active → Upcoming → Archived** sub-sections, each headed by a `.roadmap-details-substate` separator (short uppercase label + trailing rule; per-state accent: active green, upcoming cyan, archived dimmed tan). A bucket's separator renders only when non-empty (no orphan headers). Within a bucket entries sort by date — Active/Upcoming ascending (soonest first), Archived descending (most recent first), date-less last; date key = first present of `targetDate`/`end`/`startDate`/`start` (+`createdAt` for releases). Unknown/blank state falls to Upcoming so nothing is dropped. Entry rendering refactored into `epicEntryHtml`/`releaseEntryHtml` builders + a generic `renderStateGroups`; the redundant inline lifecycle word was dropped from the release detail body. CSS adds the dark-cockpit `.roadmap-details-substate` rule + `.roadmap-pdf-light` overrides (darkened per-state hues for AA contrast on white); PDF pagination `blockSel` gains `.roadmap-details-substate` so a separator can't orphan from its entries. All dynamic values via `escapeHtml()`. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0644: Enrich Roadmap Details & Reference entries with IDs, dates & progress (`share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters `lcars.js?v=3.49`→`?v=3.50`, `lcars.css?v=32.19`→`?v=32.20`). Frontend-only follow-on to XACA-0642 (no `/api/roadmap` payload change — all fields already present). The Epics group now lists **all** epics rather than only those carrying a description; description-less epics render a title + meta line only. Each epic entry gains a secondary `.roadmap-details-entry-meta` line showing the Epic ID, the resolved start → target dates (explicit `startDate`/`targetDate` when set, otherwise rollup `start`/`end` — matching the timeline bar-tooltip date resolution), and X/Y progress (`itemCounts.completed`/`total`) when `total` is truthy; the description body line renders only when non-empty. Release entries gain the same meta-line pattern surfacing the REL-id, with the derived detail text (type · status · state · date) kept in the body; an absent/blank release id omits the meta line entirely (no stray empty div). CSS adds a dark-cockpit `.roadmap-details-entry-meta` rule plus a `.roadmap-pdf-light` override (dark text, full opacity) so the meta line stays legible on the white html2canvas+jsPDF export; PDF pagination `blockSel` is unchanged because the meta line is a child of `.roadmap-details-entry` (already one atomic pagination unit — title + meta + body never split). All interpolated values pass through `escapeHtml()`. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0642: Roadmap Details & Reference section (`share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters `lcars.js?v=3.47`→`?v=3.49`, `lcars.css?v=32.17`→`?v=32.19`). Adds a text-details reference section rendered below the Roadmap timeline + unscheduled lanes (in `renderRoadmap()`): an Epics group listing each epic with its `epic.description`, then a Releases group listing each release with a detail string derived from existing fields (type · status · state · date — no release schema change). Empty-state: the Epics group is suppressed when no epic carries a description, description-less epics are skipped, and the whole section is suppressed when there are no described epics and no releases; all interpolated text/attribute values pass through `escapeHtml()`. Server (`serve_roadmap`) needed no change — `epic.description` and the release fields were already present in the `/api/roadmap` payload for scheduled and unscheduled items. Styling: dark-cockpit `.roadmap-details*` classes (epic `data-color` / release `data-type` left-border accents) plus `.roadmap-pdf-light` light-theme overrides (dark text on light bg) for the WKWebView html2canvas+jsPDF export; the export pagination `blockSel` is extended with the new section selectors so it paginates without cutting entry cards. Follow-on to XACA-0641. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0643: Parameterized-team board creation & contract-violating bare template keys (`bin/aiteamforge-setup.sh`, `libexec/commands/kb-port-fix.py`, plus `sync-tap.sh` mirrors `share/scripts/kb-init-team-guard.sh`, `share/scripts/aiteamforge-team-paths-wizard.py`, `share/kanban-hooks/aiteamforge_paths.py`, `share/lcars-ui/server.py`). The interactive setup wizard now forwards the captured `--project`/`--client` to `install-team.sh` so parameterized templates (finance/legal/medical/freelance) build the REQUESTED instance board (e.g. `legal-coparenting`) instead of falling back to `<template>-default` — the previous miss left the team boardless, so the startup guard tried to provision and collided on the already-registered team code. The startup guard now resolves the concrete instance id generically from `team-paths.json` (table-free, replacing a brittle 3-entry map) for board detection + provisioning. `aiteamforge_paths.py` stops seeding bare `medical`/`freelance` keys (team-id contract violations the server already drops with a per-read warning) and self-heals existing `team-paths.json` files by scrubbing them (backup + atomic write); `_PARAMETERIZED_TEMPLATES` becomes the single source of truth imported by `server.py` and `kb-port-fix.py`; `kb-port-fix.py` no longer allocates ports to bare-template keys. Mirror of canonical changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0641: Roadmap timeline clipping, unreadable labels & export header (`share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters `lcars.js?v=3.41`→`?v=3.47`, `lcars.css?v=32.13`→`?v=32.17`). Fixes a cluster of Roadmap timeline rendering defects (on-screen and in the cloned PDF export): (1) first/last markers clipped — the time domain is projected into a gutter-inset `[4,96]%` (`projectPct`, shared by `dateToPct`/`dateRangeWidthPct`/axis-ticks) so edge items carry a built-in margin and stay aligned to their month ticks; the legacy post-pack edge-nudge it supersedes (which shoved the range-end marker into its neighbour) is removed; (2) bar titles squeezed to stubs (`FL`) — `min-width:0` on bar titles, sublabel always dropped from the bar face (kept in tooltip), badge dropped on narrow bars, and an external label rendered beside the bar when the title can't fit even alone (`.roadmap-bar-extlabel`); (3) PDF-only marker overlap & swapped order — stacked milestones now carry no transform and are centered by an explicit pixel `left` (html2canvas mis-renders `translateX(-50%)`); (4) `buildRoadmapPdf()` re-runs the lane stacker on the off-screen clone and the stacker retries on a zero-width pre-layout track, so row-packing/title-fit/external-labels apply in the export; (5) new export-only title header (team name + Roadmap + generation date) prepended to the PDF clone. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- Maintenance (no ticket): Restore v0.13.0 version advertisement on tap `main` (`Formula/aiteamforge.rb`, `VERSION`: `0.12.12`→`0.13.0`). Tap commit `89e393f`, authored on a concurrent session, fixed a regression where XACA-0636 commit `78cd31a` reverted the Formula tag/version back to `0.12.12` (stale working tree, broad `git add`) so `brew upgrade` saw the tap as up-to-date and would not install the published `v0.13.0` tag. No git tag moved — brew installs the content at tag `v0.13.0`. Documented here because this PR (XACA-0640) is the first to advance develop's submodule pointer past `89e393f`; its subject carries no XACA id to match, so the tap-changelog-completeness gate is bypassed for it via a `Changelog-Skip:` trailer (see the outer pointer-bump commit).
- XACA-0640: Roadmap PDF light-theme schedule-badge contrast (`share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-buster `lcars.css?v=32.12`→`?v=32.13`). The `ROLLUP`/`ACTUAL`/`EXPLICIT`/`EST` schedule-source pills were near-invisible on the white PDF export — their dark-cockpit styling uses translucent pale-on-pale fills tuned to sit subdued against dark bars (rollup ~1.7:1, activity ~2.0:1 on white, below the WCAG 3:1 floor) and the `.roadmap-pdf-light` export block never re-skinned them. Added light-theme overrides re-skinning each badge to a solid light tint + dark saturated text while preserving source semantics (cyan=explicit, amber=derived, blue=rollup, green=activity); all combos now clear WCAG AA (4.5:1+). Also bumped chip-meta to full opacity on the light card for crisp archived/maintenance metadata. CSS-only, scoped to the transient export clone — no on-screen cockpit change. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0639: Roadmap archived-Release activity span — archived Releases now surface a historical bar on the scheduled timeline instead of always dropping to the unscheduled Archived bucket (`share/lcars-ui/server.py`, `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-buster `lcars.js?v=3.39`→`?v=3.41`). Follow-up to XACA-0638/0635 (EPIC-0040). `serve_roadmap` builds a release→items index from the inverse pointer `item.releaseAssignment.releaseId` (releases carry no itemIds), then a new `_schedule_archived_release(release)` helper derives the span: PRIMARY = item-activity rollup (reuses `_activity_rollup`, now parameterized with `extend_to_today=True` default so epic behavior is unchanged) over the release's assigned items with `extend_to_today=False` (archived releases are historical — never clamp to today even with open items); FALLBACK = the release's own `createdAt → archivedAt` lifecycle span when no completed-item timestamps exist; releases with neither stay unscheduled. Emits `scheduleSource='activity'`, and the release timeline-bar render path now renders the muted-green `ACTUAL` badge (previously releases had no schedule-source badge). Applied to BOTH archived-release sites (on-board `status='archived'` + the `_load_archived_releases` archive-dir loop); dedup-by-id preserved. Archived releases with a derived span render as a ranged timeline **bar** (start→end, `widthPct`) rather than a point milestone, so the historical extent is visible (mirrors the epic lane). Scope: archived releases only — non-archived releases keep their `targetDate` point-event milestone behavior. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0636: Fixed/Changed — Roadmap timeline polish + PDF export rework (`share/lcars-ui/server.py`, `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters `lcars.js?v=3.32`→`?v=3.39`, `lcars.css?v=32.8`→`?v=32.12`). Scheduled-timeline lanes now row-stack overlapping epics/releases instead of overlapping them (html2canvas-safe pixel positioning); axis/milestone edge labels no longer clip; narrow bars drop their sublabel so the title isn't truncated. The unscheduled Epics lane buckets by the epic's own `status` (new server `_derive_epic_roadmap_state` — planning→Planned/active→Active/completed→Archived) so planning epics stop landing in Active; release chips are natural-sorted so semver orders correctly. The PDF export is reworked: it captures an off-screen clone (so WKWebView lays out the full content — the live overflow:auto container left lower rows unrendered, truncating the PDF), uses chunked gap-aware pagination (no truncation, no card sliced across a break), explicit 1122×793 landscape pages, a white light theme, and outer margins with flush internal joins. The export now downloads via a new server endpoint (`POST /api/roadmap-pdf` + `GET /api/roadmap-pdf/<token>`) that streams it with a `Content-Disposition` filename — the only mechanism the WKWebView cockpit honors — so it saves as `<Team> - Roadmap - YYYYMMDD.pdf`. Mirror of canonical lcars-ui changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0638: Roadmap activity-rollup scheduling tier — derive epic start/end from child item activity timestamps when no explicit dates or child dueDates exist (`share/lcars-ui/server.py`, `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters bumped `lcars.js?v=3.31`→`?v=3.32`, `lcars.css?v=32.7`→`?v=32.8`). Follow-up to XACA-0627/0635 (EPIC-0040): only ~3.7% of completed items carry a `dueDate` but ~99% have a `completedAt`, so epics full of finished work with no due dates fell to the unscheduled lane despite having a concrete historical span. `serve_roadmap` gains a shared `_activity_rollup` helper (with a `_date_part` ISO-timestamp→`YYYY-MM-DD` truncator) that derives a span from actual work history: **start = MIN(`startedAt`|`addedAt`), end = MAX(`completedAt`)** across non-cancelled children that have `completedAt`; it emits `scheduleSource='activity'`. The helper is invoked from two sites — the new Rule 5.5 (between the child-dueDate rollup and the unscheduled fallback) for ACTIVE epics, and the ARCHIVED branch — so the logic lives in exactly one place. ACTIVE mixed-state epics clamp end to today (EXTEND-TO-TODAY) while work continues; ARCHIVED (fully-completed) epics have no open children so their end stays at `max(completedAt)`, an honest historical finish. ARCHIVED epics now surface an actual/historical bar instead of always dropping to the Archived bucket (refines XACA-0635), but still never receive *planned* bars (explicit/derived/dueDate-rollup). The Roadmap renders a muted-green `ACTUAL` schedule-source badge distinguishing actual/historical spans from planned rollups. Only truly date-less, work-less epics remain unscheduled. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`.

## [0.13.0] - 2026-06-05

- XACA-0627: Roadmap Phase 2 — explicit time-estimate & due-date scheduling for Epics (`share/lcars-ui/server.py`, `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters bumped `lcars.css?v=32.6`→`?v=32.7`, `lcars.js?v=3.30`→`?v=3.31`). Phase 2 of EPIC-0040, building on the XACA-0625 Roadmap tab: epics may now carry explicit `startDate`/`targetDate`/`timeEstimate` fields, and `serve_roadmap` positions each epic by those explicit dates instead of always rolling up child-item due dates. Resolution precedence (non-archived epics only): **explicit range (start+target) → derived (start + parsed `timeEstimate`) → explicit point (start-only or target-only) → child-dueDate rollup → unscheduled**; the payload gains a `scheduleSource` field (`explicit`/`derived`/`rollup`) plus the raw fields, explicit dates fold into the timeline `dateRange`, and explicitly-dated childless epics now land in the scheduled lane. ARCHIVED epics remain in the unscheduled Archived bucket regardless of explicit dates (preserves XACA-0635). New `_parse_duration` helper (`Nh`/`Nd`/`Nw`/`Nm`, 30-day month). The Roadmap view renders a schedule-source badge (`EXPLICIT`/`EST`/`ROLLUP`) and a `timeEstimate` label on epic bars/milestones (additive — positioning math unchanged — print/PDF-safe). All fields optional/backward-compatible. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`. The `kb-epic` setter commands (`set-start-date`/`set-target-date`/`set-estimate`, create flags, `update` allowlist) live in the dev-only `kanban-helpers.sh` and are not tap-shipped.
- XACA-0635: Changed — Roadmap "Unscheduled" lane now sub-groups each kind (Epics, Releases) by state: Planned / Active / Archived (`share/lcars-ui/server.py`, `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters bumped `lcars.js?v=3.29`→`?v=3.30`, `lcars.css?v=32.5`→`?v=32.6`). Building on XACA-0634's kind split, chips inside each kind group are now divided into Planned/Active/Archived sub-rows using the existing canonical state definitions, so the roadmap agrees with the Epics and Releases tabs: epic state derives from child-item states (`_derive_epic_state`, STATE_CONTRACT §1.5), release state mirrors the Releases-tab split (XACA-0238) via a new `_derive_release_state` helper. The server now also loads archived releases (`_load_archived_releases`) into the roadmap payload — previously dropped entirely — deduped by id, so the Releases Archived bucket can populate. Archived epics/releases are always placed in the unscheduled Archived bucket regardless of date (kept out of the scheduled timeline). Each unscheduled payload carries a `state` field; empty buckets are not rendered; unknown/missing state falls back to Active so no chip is dropped. Mirror of canonical lcars-ui changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0634: Changed — Roadmap "Unscheduled" lane now splits Epics and Releases into separate labeled groups (`share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters bumped `lcars.js?v=3.28`→`?v=3.29`, `lcars.css?v=32.4`→`?v=32.5`). Previously all unscheduled items rendered in one flat chip list under a single "Unscheduled" header; a chip's border/label color encodes the epic color or release type — never the kind — so an Epic and a Release could look identical (e.g. both blue). The lane now renders two `.roadmap-unscheduled-group` subsections each with a `.roadmap-unscheduled-subheading` ("Releases" first, then "Epics"), mirroring the timeline's "Epics"/"Releases" lane labels and color-matched to them (releases = green, epics = purple); empty groups are not rendered. Mirror of canonical lcars-ui changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0633: Bugfix — Roadmap "Export PDF" reworked to client-side generation (`share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`, new vendored `share/lcars-ui/js/vendor/html2canvas.min.js` + `share/lcars-ui/js/vendor/jspdf.umd.min.js`; cache-buster bumped `lcars.js?v=3.26`→`?v=3.28`). `window.print()` is a no-op in iTerm2's WKWebView cockpit — Export added the `body.roadmap-printing` scope but no dialog opened and `afterprint` never fired, leaving a stuck full-window takeover with no PDF. `exportRoadmapPdf()` now captures `#roadmap-content` with html2canvas and builds a landscape PDF with jsPDF (vendored locally — no CDN/server deps), downloaded via `doc.save()` with a new-tab `bloburl` fallback for WebViews that don't honor `<a download>`. JPEG-embedded (jsPDF 2.5.1's PNG parser rejects some html2canvas output). Legacy `body.roadmap-printing`/`@media print` CSS left intact (harmless). Verified by generating a real PDF headlessly. Mirror of canonical lcars-ui changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0630: Estimate-vs-Actual Handicap Analytics LCARS surface — a new HOME "Estimation Accuracy" carousel panel comparing effort estimates (`points` = developer-hours) against actual active-work time (`timeWorkedMs`) on completed kanban items to derive an average correction factor ("handicap"), globally and by estimate-size bucket (`<=1h`/`1-4h`/`4-8h`/`>8h`). New `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars-estimates.js` (self-contained module: fetches `GET /api/estimates`, renders global weighted handicap + median + excluded-reason tally + 4-bucket table, polished empty-state when `eligible==0`/`handicap==null`, no client-side recompute) plus the new server endpoint `_build_estimates_payload`/`serve_estimates` wired into `do_GET` (`share/lcars-ui/server.py`), carousel wiring in `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js` (`TOTAL_HOME_PANELS` 6→7, new `case 6`, RAG-panel offset shift) and `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`. Cache-busters bumped in `share/lcars-ui/index.html`: `js/lcars.js?v=3.25`→`?v=3.26`, `css/lcars.css?v=32.3`→`?v=32.4`, new `js/lcars-estimates.js?v=1.0`. The Python endpoint matches the canonical spec (`kanban/plans/XACA-0630/handicap-spec.md`) byte-for-byte — banker's-rounding parity with the dev-side `kb-variance` jq CLI — and ships with an anti-drift parity guard (`share/lcars-ui/tests/test_xaca0630_parity.py`, 38 tests: live-board CLI==server, empty-state fixture, §7.2 worked example, bucket boundaries, excluded-reason classification). Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`. The `kb-variance` CLI itself lives in the dev-only `kanban-helpers.sh` (not tap-shipped).
- XACA-0629: Bugfix — Roadmap "Export PDF" rendered a blank page (`share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-buster bumped `lcars.css?v=32.1`→`?v=32.3`). The `body.roadmap-printing` print scope failed to override the base `.lcars-content > .roadmap-section { position:absolute !important }` (un-`!important` `position:static` lost on specificity), so the section stayed absolute with `top:0;bottom:0` on an auto-height parent and collapsed to 0px → empty print output. Fix raises the print selector specificity (`body.roadmap-printing .lcars-content > .roadmap-section`) + `!important` + offset resets (also resetting the `.active` `overflow-y:auto` via `overflow-y:visible`); resets `min-height:0` on the section's ancestor chain plus an explicit `body.roadmap-printing #roadmap-content { min-height:0 }` override so the content area is content-driven; and duplicates the layout-reset block inside `@media print`. Verified by rendering the print output to PDF. Mirror of canonical lcars-ui changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0628: Purge the per-client/project freelance roster from the tap's registries — generic-freelance routing only. The 9 per-CLIENT/PROJECT freelance slugs (DoubleNode/Liquidstyle/Bandwear → FSW/FAP/FWS/FLB/VAN/FAS/FLA/FLI/BWA) are removed from the tap shell registry `libexec/lib/aiteamforge-paths.sh` (the SHELL twin of `aiteamforge_paths.py`'s `DEFAULT_TEAMS`, edited directly as it is tap-canonical with no dev-team source) and from the mirrored `share/kanban-hooks/aiteamforge_paths.py` (canonical edit upstream in XACA-0628). These slugs now live solely in the per-machine overlay (`~/.aiteamforge/team-paths.json`), which merges its team_codes on top of the defaults; the generic `freelance` (FRE) entry STAYS as the universal fallback. Mirror of the canonical `lcars-ui/`, `kanban-hooks/`, and `scripts/` routing edits via `sync-tap.sh` (`share/lcars-ui/server.py`, `share/kanban-hooks/{aiteamforge_paths.py,kanban_utils.py,kanban-hook.py,test_aiteamforge_paths.py}`, `share/scripts/{lcars-tmp-dir.sh,kb-init-team,kanban-backup.py}`). No legitimate per-client config files (XACA-0535 allowlisted `freelance-<client>-*.yaml`) are touched.
- XACA-0625: LCARS Roadmap tab — aggregate Epics & Releases into an exportable timeline view with print/PDF export (`share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`, `share/lcars-ui/server.py`; cache-busters bumped `lcars.js?v=3.23`→`?v=3.25`, `lcars.css?v=32.0`→`?v=32.1`). Mirror of canonical lcars-ui changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0624: LCARS editable effort-**points** pill for the kanban "developer-hours points" feature (`share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`; cache-busters bumped in `share/lcars-ui/index.html`: `js/lcars.js?v=3.23` → `?v=3.24`, `css/lcars.css?v=32.0` → `?v=32.1`). Item cards render a `points` pill — cyan `<n>h` when estimated, muted dashed `—` when unestimated (predicate `item.points != null && typeof item.points === 'number' && item.points >= 0`); clicking a non-completed item's pill opens a numeric editor popup (presets + Clear, mirroring the due-date editor) that POSTs `updates.points` / `clearFields:['points']` to the existing generic `/api/update-item` handler; the expanded detail metrics row gains an "Estimate: Nh" entry. Mirror of the canonical `lcars-ui/` changes (`sync-tap.sh`). The enforcement/CLI side (`kanban-helpers.sh` start-gate + `kb-backlog points`/`unestimated`) and the migration script are dev-only and not tap-shipped.
- XACA-0617: Container-root fallback for `deploy-worktree-personas.sh --all` backfill (`share/scripts/deploy-worktree-personas.sh`). `--all <team> [<main_repo_path>]` silently reported "nothing to backfill" when given a *container* path (e.g. `MainEventApp-iOS/`) because the container is not itself a git repo and `git worktree list` returned nothing — the same container-vs-git-root false-negative class as XACA-0606 (which fixed the dev-only `kb-sync-personas`). New `_resolve_git_roots <path>` helper (twin of XACA-0606's; container-if-git else immediate-child git repos, handles DNS-style multi-inner-repo umbrellas) resolves the real git root(s) before `_deploy_all` enumerates worktrees: a plain repo path yields one root (no regression), a container yields its inner repo(s), and a container with no inner git repo emits a clear warning + exits 0 (benign). The `$PWD` default no longer hard-fails when run from a container cwd. Loop-locals hoisted (k501). Selftest 16 → 19 (container single-root, DNS multi-root, empty-container). Tap-mirrored via `sync-tap.sh`.

- XACA-0615: Fix bash-4 `${var,,}` "bad substitution" crash in `share/scripts/kb-init-team-guard.sh`. `kb_ensure_team_initialized()` used bash-4's lowercase parameter expansion in the interactive "Initialize this team now?" prompt; the guard is sourced by `<team>-startup.sh` (`#!/bin/zsh`) so it runs under zsh — where that expansion is a "bad substitution" — and also fails under macOS `/bin/bash` 3.2.57. The expansion is evaluated before the `case` matches, so any answer crashed the prompt. Replaced with portable case-insensitive bracket globs + in-loop normalization to a canonical `y`/`n`. Only fired when the board was empty/missing AND a TTY was present (fresh/headless install); discovered running `finance-startup.sh` on a freshly-installed tap host. Canonical mirror via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0611: Harden imgcat provisioning — bundle vendored imgcat in `share/iterm2/imgcat` (canonical: `scripts/vendor/iterm2/imgcat`; sourced from iterm2.com/utilities/imgcat, 8560 bytes, shebang `#!/usr/bin/env bash`). New shared helper `libexec/lib/imgcat-provision.sh` exports `ensure_imgcat <share_dir>`: deterministic bundled-copy primary, hardened `curl -fsSL` iterm2.com fallback (`-f` so an HTTP error never lands an empty/bogus file that would pass verify as a fake imgcat), file+exec+non-empty verify, idempotent no-op guard, `rm -f` cleanup of any partial artifact on total failure; returns non-zero on failure so callers emit loud warnings. `sync-tap.sh` extended with `sync_file` entry for `scripts/vendor/iterm2/imgcat → share/iterm2/imgcat`. Install-side wiring (`libexec/installers/install-shell.sh`): source `imgcat-provision.sh` after `common.sh`; remove old silent `curl … imgcat … 2>/dev/null` block (unverified, network-only, skipped when shell integration pre-existed); call `ensure_imgcat "$INSTALL_ROOT/share"` unconditionally after the shell-integration if/else so imgcat is provisioned on BOTH the fresh-install and already-present branches (closes the M1Pro/fleet blank-panel root cause). Non-fatal: imgcat failure emits a loud `warning` but does not abort the install. Call-site comment documents the SSH-delegated remote-host requirement (cockpit connect runs `agent-panel-display.sh` on the remote — remote host needs its own `~/.iterm2/imgcat`). Troubleshooting note added to `docs/TROUBLESHOOTING.md` § "Agent Panel Images Blank". Upgrade-side wiring (`libexec/commands/aiteamforge-upgrade.sh`): source `imgcat-provision.sh` after `common.sh`; new `update_imgcat()` follows the `update_*` conventions (section header, success/failure logging, dry-run no-op); non-fatal because a non-zero `ensure_imgcat` return is consumed by the `if ensure_imgcat …; then … else … fi` construct (never trips `set -eo pipefail`); wired into the main upgrade call sequence between `update_runtime_helpers` and `update_shell_helpers`. On failure emits three `print_warning` lines explaining that agent-panel images will not render and pointing at the expected location and manual fix. Imgcat provisioning is NOT part of the `update_runtime_helpers` sweep (share/scripts/) — the vendored asset lives under `share/iterm2/` and is managed exclusively by `ensure_imgcat`.

- XACA-0603: Add a public CLI write path for the CR field `deploy_window_planned` in `kb-cr` (`share/scripts/kb-cr.sh` in tap). Previously `kb-cr` only READ `deploy_window_planned` (show, summary, audit, LCARS) with no public verb to WRITE it, forcing operators to hand-edit `board.json`. Adds (1) a `--deploy-window <date>` flag on `kb-cr create` and (2) a post-hoc `kb-cr reschedule <CR-ID> <date>` setter verb. Both write `.crs[].deploy_window_planned` UTC-normalized via a new shared, BSD-`date`-safe `_kb_cr_normalize_iso_date` helper (accepts `YYYY-MM-DD` → `T00:00:00Z`, `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MMZ` → padded, `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ` → pass-through; rejects offset/garbage forms), bump `updatedAt`/`lastUpdated`, and emit a best-effort `cr_deploy_window_set` activity-log event.
- XACA-0602: Fix two LCARS team-transfer IMPORT UX defects in `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js` (cache-buster bumped in `share/lcars-ui/index.html`: `js/lcars.js?v=3.21` → `?v=3.23`). (1) **Silent re-import failure** — after a completed (or failed) import, a second attempt landed in half-dirty state because `onImportComplete()`/`onImportFailed()` re-enabled the button and cleared the file input but never reset session state: module-level `currentImportJobId` stayed pinned to the finished job, `#import-result`/`#import-preflight` were never hidden, and `currentImportSecretsDiscovered`/`stagedImportSecretsFile` stayed polluted. `cancelImport()` held the only full-reset path and was wired solely to the CANCEL button, so success/failure never routed through it. Fix: a single-source-of-truth `resetImportState()` helper (nulls the job id + staged files, `stopImportPolling()`, hides all three panels, re-arms the file input + button, clears the inline-secrets section); `cancelImport()` collapses to a thin wrapper over it; `handleImportFileSelected()` calls it at the top so selecting a new file wipes any prior import's panel + state (the re-import fix). `onImportComplete()`/`onImportFailed()` clear only the blocking bits (`currentImportJobId = null`, `stopImportPolling()`) and leave the completion panel visible per the chosen UX. (2) **No progress indicator on file select** — `uploadImportFile()` awaited one fat `/api/import/upload` POST (iCloud materialize + upload + manifest verify, all server-side) showing only a disabled button. Fix: an indeterminate `PREPARING IMPORT…` state on the reused `#import-progress` panel shown synchronously on file selection, cleared when `renderImportPreflight()` paints or on any upload error path. No DOM changes (existing panel reused). (3) Follow-up (XACA-0602-012, review): promoted `_applyPairedSecretsImport()`'s formerly function-local secrets-poll handle to a module-scoped `pairedSecretsPollingInterval` now cancelled by `stopImportPolling()`/`resetImportState()`, closing the orphaned-interval race where selecting a new file mid-secrets-extraction could leave a poll alive that re-shows the result panel. Mirror of canonical lcars-ui changes.
- XACA-0608: `aiteamforge upgrade` now refreshes per-team launch scripts. New `update_team_scripts()` in `aiteamforge-upgrade.sh` (wired into the run sequence between `update_aux_scripts` and `update_shell_helpers`) refreshes the per-team `<team>-startup.sh` / `<team>-shutdown.sh` and the station scripts under `<team>/scripts/*.sh` from `share/scripts/teams/` into the working dir — the files `install-team.sh`'s parametric branch (XACA-0483/0484) lays down but the upgrade path never refreshed. Before this fix the working-dir copies were FROZEN at install-time: a `brew upgrade` that shipped a newer `<team>-startup.sh` to the Cellar left the runtime copy stale (e.g. M1Pro on 0.12.12 still ran the old `cksum` LCARS-port logic — 0 occurrences of `resolve_lcars_port` — because the XACA-0590 resolver-equipped script was never laid down). Same refresh-gap class as XACA-0558 (kanban-hooks) / XACA-0585 (lcars-health) / XACA-0594 (worktree-helpers); k501 sibling-drift datapoint (install grew the team-script laydown; upgrade's refresh map never followed). Implementation: a self-maintaining LOOP over the shipped `share/scripts/teams/*-startup.sh` (a newly-added team is covered with no edit — avoids re-introducing the hardcoded-list drift); refreshes ONLY scripts already present in the working dir (does not materialise teams the user never installed — matches the `update_aux_scripts` convention); applies the SAME `~/dev-team` → `$WORKING_DIR` `sed` rewrite (incl. the `iterm2_window_manager.py` top-level special case) that install's `_xaca0483_install_script()` performs, since these scripts carry literal `~/dev-team` references and a plain `cp` would ship broken paths; preserves exec bits. These team launch scripts contain no un-rendered `{{VAR}}` template slots (verified); the path-only rewrite leaves any literal `{{VAR}}` strings (sed-substitution logic / doc comments — present in some aux/runtime helpers such as `kb-cr.sh`/`kanban-board-check.sh`) untouched, so re-rendering with the install rewrite is a faithful refresh, not a clobber. Regression test `tests/test-xaca-0608-team-script-refresh.sh` (10 assertions: refreshes installed startup with rewrite, skips non-installed teams, refreshes station scripts, preserves exec bits, dry-run no-op, missing-source graceful, wired into run sequence). Tap-native command change (homebrew-tap two-step; no `sync-tap`).
- XACA-0608 (extended): the team-script refresh above did not close the root cause on its own — the per-team `<team>-startup.sh` was refreshed, but the **top-level `share/scripts/*` helpers it sources were not**. Most critically `lcars-launch-helpers.sh`, which *defines* `resolve_lcars_port` (XACA-0590): install lays it into `$AITEAMFORGE_DIR/scripts/` (via `install-team.sh::_xaca0483_install_script`, with the `~/dev-team`→`$AITEAMFORGE_DIR` rewrite), but no upgrade function refreshed it. A machine installed before that helper gained the resolver kept a FROZEN copy with no `resolve_lcars_port`, so every `<team>-startup.sh` fell through to the `cksum`-derived port and served LCARS on the WRONG port even after a `brew upgrade`. New `update_runtime_helpers()` (wired between `update_team_scripts` and `update_shell_helpers`) closes this with a SELF-MAINTAINING sweep over every shipped `share/scripts/*.sh`, `*.py` and the extensionless `kb-init-team`, targeting `$WORKING_DIR/scripts/<name>`: refreshes ONLY targets already present (upgrade never materialises files a given machine's install layout didn't lay down — e.g. `cr-confluence-poller.py` only if CR pollers were set up); renders through the SAME rewrite-aware helper install uses (`_xaca0608_render_team_script`), correctly handling both rewrite-carrying helpers (`lcars-launch-helpers.sh` 10 refs, `agent-panel-display.sh` 4, `kb-init-team-guard.sh`, `lcars-tmp-dir.sh`, `fleet-reporter.sh`/`cr-confluence-poller.py` scripts-dir copies) and plain ones in one code path. Additionally, `update_aux_scripts()` is now rewrite-aware too — it previously `cp`'d its files WITHOUT the rewrite, so its rewrite-carrying members (`kb-cr.sh` 6 refs, `deploy-worktree-personas.sh` 2, `lcars-health-check.sh`, `worktree-helpers.sh`) were shipping UN-rewritten on upgrade; routing the map through `_xaca0608_render_team_script` (no-op for plain files) closes that latent gap. One owner per file: `update_runtime_helpers` EXCLUDES the aux-map's `scripts/`-destined basenames, and that exclusion set is DERIVED from the canonical `_xaca0608_aux_script_map` (extracted to a single source-of-truth emitter so the two can't drift). Regression test extended to 21 assertions (stale `lcars-launch-helpers.sh` gains `resolve_lcars_port`; rewrite applied [no literal `~/dev-team`, install-dir base present]; exec bit preserved; non-installed helper not materialised; aux-owned `kb-cr.sh` left untouched [no double-refresh]; derived exclusion set correct; dry-run no-op; missing-`scripts/` graceful; wired into run sequence; `update_aux_scripts` uses the rewrite-aware render). Known remaining gap filed as **XACA-0610**: the separate `fleet-monitor/client/fleet-reporter.sh` copy (install-fleet-monitor destination, not `scripts/`) is still refreshed by no upgrade function — out of scope here. Tap-native command change (homebrew-tap two-step; no `sync-tap`).

## [0.12.12] - 2026-06-02

- XACA-0601: Remove 200-item cap on manifest probe `item_ids` in `lcars-ui/team_transfer/domain_kanban.py` (`share/lcars-ui/team_transfer/domain_kanban.py` in tap). The `[:200]` slice caused a false DATA-LOSS preflight failure on any kanban board with more than 200 items — the verifier diffed the truncated source-item set against the full live board and flagged the uncapped tail as destination-only items an import would delete.
- XACA-0600: Anchor the LCARS-UI upgrade exclude so `team_transfer/config/` ships on upgrade. `aiteamforge-upgrade.sh::update_lcars()` rsynced `share/lcars-ui` → `~/aiteamforge/lcars-ui` with an unanchored `--exclude 'config/'`, intended to preserve the user-customized top-level runtime dir `lcars-ui/config/`. Because the pattern had no leading slash it matched `config/` at ANY depth, so it also stripped the SHIPPED data dir `lcars-ui/team_transfer/config/` (the per-team `.yaml` definitions that drive team import/export — currently 20 entries: 17 regular files + 3 intra-dir symlink aliases such as `medical.yaml` → `medical-general.yaml`). Fresh installs (`install-kanban.sh` `cp -r`) were unaffected; only upgrades dropped it, leaving upgrade-only machines (e.g. M1Pro: 0 yaml vs 20 in share) unable to export/import — every attempt died with `No team_transfer config for team X`. Fix: anchor the exclude to the transfer root (`--exclude '/config/'`) so only `lcars-ui/config/` is excluded while `team_transfer/config/` syncs normally. Adds a non-fatal post-sync regression guard that warns when the installed `team_transfer/config` yaml count diverges from the framework share. Tap-native installer change (homebrew-tap two-step; no sync-tap).
- XACA-0596: Replace hardcoded `SESSION_DIRECTORY` paths with `${AITEAMFORGE_DIR:-$HOME/dev-team}` in the four tap-shipping LCARS startup scripts (`share/scripts/teams/finance/scripts/finance-lcars-startup.sh`, `freelance/scripts/freelance-lcars-startup.sh`, `legal/scripts/legal-lcars-startup.sh`, `medical/scripts/medical-lcars-startup.sh`). These files shipped a literal `/Users/darrenehlers/dev-team` (or the half-portable `$HOME/dev-team` for finance) as the `SESSION_DIRECTORY` default, causing them to point at the wrong directory on any tap install that uses a non-default `AITEAMFORGE_DIR`. The portable form honors `$AITEAMFORGE_DIR` when set and falls back to `$HOME/dev-team` otherwise — identical behavior on the dev machine.
- XACA-0596: Also make the `lcars-ports` port/theme/order rendezvous directory portable in the tap-shipping fleet scripts. `$HOME/dev-team/lcars-ports` → `${AITEAMFORGE_DIR:-$HOME/dev-team}/lcars-ports` in the team startup/shutdown producers and the consumers `fleet-reporter.sh` and `agent-panel-display.sh`. The rendezvous dir is a producer/consumer contract — both ends must resolve to the same path, so on a tap install with a non-default `AITEAMFORGE_DIR` the producers (writing `.port`/`.theme`/`.order` files) and consumers (reading them) now stay in agreement instead of split-braining. Identical behavior when `AITEAMFORGE_DIR` is unset.
- XACA-0592: Re-sync `share/scripts/` files that had drifted stale because they were never in the `sync-tap.sh` mirror map (follow-up to XACA-0591). Affected: the 4 parametric teams' (finance/freelance/legal/medical) `share/scripts/teams/<team>-startup.sh` + `teams/<team>/scripts/*` station scripts, now carrying forward-fixes that the false-green drift had stranded (XACA-0576 board-check template id, XACA-0590 canonical `resolve_lcars_port`, XACA-0279 active-account banner). `update_claude_agent.sh` newly mapped (was already in sync). No behavior change to tap-only files (`aiteamforge-lcars.json`, `aiteamforge-resolve-hostname.sh`, `create-lcars-profile.py`, `init-agent-panel-json.py`, `kanban-board-check.sh`, `kanban-restore-helper.sh`, `register-terminals.sh`) — those have no dev-team canonical and remain tap-authoritative. Exec bits preserved.
- XACA-0594: Add `worktree-helpers.sh` to `update_aux_scripts()` script_map in `aiteamforge-upgrade.sh`. The file is installed to `${AITEAMFORGE_DIR}/worktree-helpers.sh` (root) by `install_worktree_helpers()` during fresh installs and uninstalled by `aiteamforge-uninstall.sh`, but the upgrade path omitted it — meaning the working-dir copy was frozen at the version shipped when the user first ran `aiteamforge setup`. Tap upgrades never refreshed it, so features added in later releases (XACA-0588 wt-new persona-deploy hook, XACA-0565 board-routing consolidation) were silently absent on upgraded machines. The new entry mirrors the target path convention used by install-shell.sh: `${WORKING_DIR}/worktree-helpers.sh`.
- XACA-0598: `share/scripts/worktree-helpers.sh` gains `_wt_classify_branch` — a shared safe/unsafe branch classifier (tokens `merged` / `unmerged-commits` / `remote-gone`) extracted from `wt-finish`'s inline logic, so the new kb-run worktree-cleanup-on-exit offer and `wt-finish` agree on what is safe to remove. `wt-finish` refactored to delegate classification to it (no user-facing behavior change). The cleanup-offer driver (`_kb_offer_worktree_cleanup`) and its wiring into the `kb-run*` launchers live in `kanban-helpers.sh`, which is dev-only and not tap-shipped; only the shared classifier in `worktree-helpers.sh` mirrors to the tap.

## [0.12.11] - 2026-06-01

- XACA-0593: Wire persona-deploy hook into tap-native `wt-create()` in `share/templates/aliases/worktree-aliases.sh`. XACA-0588 added the hook only to `wt-new()` in `worktree-helpers.sh` (the dev-machine path). On tap machines `worktree-helpers.sh` is not sourced and `wt-new` does not exist — `wt-create` is the only worktree-creation path. After the successful `git worktree add`, a guarded call to `${AITEAMFORGE_DIR}/scripts/deploy-worktree-personas.sh "$worktree_dir" "$WT_CURRENT_PROJECT"` deploys team personas into the new worktree's `.claude/agents/`. The call is on the success path only, wrapped in `[ -x "$_dwp" ]` and `|| true` so it never aborts worktree creation. Variables proven in-scope: `$worktree_dir` (computed above the guard, holds the new worktree absolute path) and `$WT_CURRENT_PROJECT` (exported by `wt-project`). Upgrade path: `aiteamforge-upgrade.sh::update_alias_files()` already refreshes `share/aliases/worktree-aliases.sh` when the tap source is newer — existing tap machines receive the hook on next `aiteamforge upgrade` with no additional wiring needed. **Follow-up (layout-agnostic guard):** `share/scripts/deploy-worktree-personas.sh` — the `_guard_worktree_target` function required the worktree to live under `<repo>/worktrees/` (dev layout only). Tap/container machines create worktrees at `dirname(repo)/worktrees/` via `wt-create` (sibling layout), causing the guard to reject every persona deploy with `ERROR: Worktree target does not live under repo worktrees/ dir`. Guard now checks git-registry membership: parses `git worktree list --porcelain` from the main repo root and accepts any path that appears as a registered linked worktree (not the main/primary entry). Layout-agnostic — git tracks linked worktrees regardless of position relative to the main repo. `--all` mode also updated: the `worktrees_dir` prefix filter removed; it now enumerates all linked worktrees from git directly. Selftest expanded to 16 tests (real `git worktree add` repos in both dev and sibling layouts).

## [0.12.10] - 2026-06-01

Mirror of the canonical `scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh` change; the dev-team startup scripts that adopt it are not tap-shipped, but the shared helper is, so the tap copy must carry the resolver too. Fixes tracked `lcars-ports/*.port` files drifting on every launch when the runtime hash disagreed with the reconciled canonical port.
- XACA-0588: Tap-machine worktree persona deployment. `share/scripts/deploy-worktree-personas.sh` added to tap mirror; `install_worktree_personas_script()` in `install-kanban.sh` seeds it to `$AITEAMFORGE_DIR/scripts/` with `chmod +x` so the wt-new `-x` guard resolves it at runtime; `update_aux_scripts` script_map in `aiteamforge-upgrade.sh` keeps it current on upgrade. Without this install seeding, the worktree persona deployment feature (XACA-0584) was a complete silent no-op on tap machines. Review follow-ups (PR #504): `--all` backfill mode added to `deploy-worktree-personas.sh` (`_deploy_core()` extracted for DRY sharing; `_deploy_all()` enumerates `git worktree list --porcelain`; selftest 12→14 tests covering --all happy-path and no-worktrees no-op); regression test `tests/test-xaca-0588-deploy-worktree-personas-laydown.sh` added (11 tests: tap ships script +x, installer lays down to scripts/ +x, hook guard path, upgrade script_map, idempotent, missing-source graceful); tests/README.md coverage table updated; sync-tap.sh comment corrected from XACA-0584 to XACA-0588.
- XACA-0586: import-preflight gate part 2 (XACA-0583 follow-up). `share/lcars-ui/team_transfer/verifier.py` gains a `STALE-OK` informational disposition: in `--phase pre-import`, present-but-stale carried payload (EXACT sha mismatch; SCHEMA board behind source, `missing = cap_ids - cur_ids`) → STALE-OK instead of FAIL, because an overwrite-import simply refreshes it. Adds real data-loss detection — `_verify_board_schema` computes `extra = cur_ids - cap_ids` (destination-only items an overwrite would DELETE) → `FAIL` with a `DATA-LOSS:` prefix in BOTH phases (checked before `missing`, so data-loss wins). Post-restore semantics unchanged (all reclassification `phase==PRE_IMPORT`-gated). `share/lcars-ui/team_transfer/manifest.py`/`generator.py` embed `source_team` so `share/lcars-ui/server.py` can block a wrong-team import (base-vs-base compare; legacy empty `source_team` skips gracefully). server.py parses the `STALE-OK: N` summary line into `verifierSummary.staleOk`. Mirror of canonical lcars-ui changes; regression tests mirrored under `share/lcars-ui/tests/team_transfer/` (208 passing).
- XACA-0582: import-apply preflight-delta override — `acknowledgePreflightDeltas` operator in `share/lcars-ui/server.py` unblocks migration import-apply when preflight FAILs are expected payload deltas (path-map drift, pending-import files). LCARS UI (`share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`) gains the corresponding toggle control. Mirror of canonical lcars-ui changes.
- XACA-0585: Ship `lcars-health-check.sh` through the tap install/upgrade pipeline. `share/scripts/lcars-health-check.sh` added; `install_lcars_health_check_script()` in `install-kanban.sh` lays it down before `install_lcars_health_launchagent`; `update_aux_scripts` script_map in `aiteamforge-upgrade.sh` keeps it current on upgrade; `aiteamforge-doctor` gains a missing-script check. Fixes exit 127 on the `com.aiteamforge.lcars-health` LaunchAgent (StartInterval 300) that caused dead LCARS servers to never auto-restart. The plist now invokes the script via `/bin/zsh` (was `/bin/bash`) — the script is `#!/bin/zsh` and uses zsh-only syntax (`typeset -A`, `(N)` glob), so an explicit `/bin/bash` interpreter overrode the shebang and exited 2 (parse error) on every tick; the regression test now parse-checks the shipped script under its declared interpreter. Doctor's missing-script fix-hint points at `aiteamforge setup` (not `upgrade`, which skips absent targets).

## [0.12.8] - 2026-05-28

- XACA-0583: import-preflight trustworthiness (XACA-0581 follow-up). `share/lcars-ui/team_transfer/verifier.py` gains a `--phase {pre-import,post-restore}` flag (default `post-restore` = legacy behavior) and two informational dispositions for absent files: `PENDING-IMPORT` (carried payload absent during a pre-import audit — the import will create it, so it is not a FAIL) and `EXPECTED-MISSING` (machine-local/ephemeral state — Claude session transcripts under `.claude/projects/*.jsonl` — that can never round-trip cross-machine). Neither sets the exit code. The upload-time import-preflight in `share/lcars-ui/server.py` GATES the apply (`FAIL>0` → `baseMatch=False` → HTTP 400); it now runs `--phase pre-import` so legitimately-absent carried payload reports PENDING-IMPORT and the apply proceeds (a genuine mismatch still FAILs and blocks). All three verifier call-sites are phase-tagged (import-preflight = pre-import, post-restore import = post-restore, source-side export preflight = post-restore). Both destination call-sites surface `pendingImport`/`expectedMissing` counts. The 47 field FAILs decompose to ~19 expected-missing + ~17 pending-import + ~9 stale-manifest drift. Regression tests mirrored under `share/lcars-ui/tests/team_transfer/`.
- XACA-0581: import-preflight follow-up to XACA-0580. (1) `kanban-hooks/aiteamforge_paths.py` `build_import_path_maps()`: skip the per-team map when `src_wd == dev-team root` — academy/freelance both report `working_dir == ~/dev-team` and were emitting maps that collide with the shared-infra `~/dev-team`→`~/aiteamforge` map (equal prefix length → stable-sort tiebreak; the academy map was dead-by-luck and actively wrong). (2) `share/lcars-ui/team_transfer/verifier.py`: SKIP the `aiteamforge_product` channel (installer-owned files the tap lays down with its own subdir layout, not carried by the transfer; a flat prefix path-map cannot bridge the reshape, so FAILing on them is a false negative). Removed the now-unreachable `aiteamforge_product` channel-class invariant. Verified live on M1Pro: 73→47 FAILs (all 26 in-scope false negatives eliminated). Regression tests mirrored under `share/lcars-ui/tests/team_transfer/`.
